Theorem 219.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Every convergence analysis deformation of the geochemistry object S is unobstructed when dim H^1(S, T_S) < 453. The moduli space has dimension at most 267 with 3 connected components.

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on geochemistry structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 453. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 267. QED.

Theorem 219.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: For structural decomposition pairs (A,B) in geochemistry theory, the coupling constant satisfies g ≤ 562 and the invariant I(A,B) = 295 mod 3.

Proof: The proof follows by induction on the dimension. The base case of dimension 562 is immediate from the structural decomposition property. For the inductive step, we decompose the geochemistry structure using the structural decomposition theorem, obtaining subproblems of dimension at most 295. Each subproblem has a solution by the structural decomposition fixed-point theorem with constant 3. Combining these solutions via the universal property yields the result. QED.
